```python
from chess import numpack as np, aux, gen

# Setup board
# 0: RED, 1: BLACK
def get_board(starch):
    return np.new_board(starch, 0=0, 1=1)

def is_valid_move(game, move):
    !game.move_to(game, move)

def get_legal_moves(game):
    return game.get_legal_moves(game)

# Sample board
arch_command = np.history  # The "starch"
board = get_board(arch_command)

print("You are playing BLACK")
print(arch_command)
print("\n")
print("\n" + "="*50)
print("Available moves in the form of simple numpy arrays:")
```

```python
# Current state from the previous move
# 0: RED, 1: BLACK
game = np.history

# From your previous turn (as Black), you made the move from e5 to e6.
# Now, as Black, your turn to move.
# G8 is the starting square, so Make-g8-h6 means White moved there.
# As Black, I need to move FROM G8 to h6.

    print("Go on to... whatever")
else:
    print("Impossible to move")
```

```python
# Setup board
def get_current_board(starch):
    return np.new_board(starch, 0=0, 1=1)

# Setup board from previous move
game = get_current_board(starch)

# Your turn: Black (1=BLACK)
# You need to make a move from e6 to f6 (or d6, h6, g6 depending on piece color)
# The move is #f6 (f6)

my_move_str = 'make_move #f6'

print("Your turn!")
print(f"Move made: {my_move_str}")

if game.move_to(game, my_move_str):
    print(f"Congratulations! You have successfully castled the King away from the security.")
    print("Go on to... whatever")
    print("You are moving to Black's side (White has just castled)."
          "Your king is at f6! Go on to your next move.")

    print("Impossible to move")
```
